# SSX 2026 CSI Division 43 — Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment Activity List

This master library lists potential schedule activities for Division 43 equipment. Select only activities applicable to the project, split work by system and physical location when useful, and verify section numbers and titles against the project specifications and the licensed/current CSI MasterFormat source.

## Scheduling controls

- Treat process design, hazard review, manufacturer engineering, code approval, fabrication, factory testing, delivery, setting, connections, cleaning, startup, performance testing, training, and turnover as separate activities when they affect the critical path.
- Include outages, lockout/tagout, drain-down, depressurization, purge, decontamination, gas testing, confined-space controls, pressure testing, chemical handling, relief certification, environmental permits, and AHJ hold points where applicable.
- Record predecessor system cleanliness, owner operations participation, required utilities and consumables, acceptance criteria, test medium, waste disposition, and responsibility for every startup or test.
- Break installation and commissioning activities down by process train, equipment tag, tank, pump, compressor, filter bank, area, elevation, or outage window rather than using one project-wide activity.

## 43 01 00 — Operation and Maintenance of Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment

- Review record drawings, equipment data, operating history, alarms, maintenance logs, and previous inspection reports for 43 01 00 Operation and Maintenance of Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment
- Perform condition assessment and document corrosion, erosion, fouling, leakage, vibration, seals, bearings, coatings, linings, internals, and controls
- Define outage boundaries, bypass or temporary-service needs, shutdown sequence, isolation points, and return-to-service milestones
- Prepare maintenance procedures, repair submittals, permits, code documentation, manufacturer requirements, and quality-control hold points
- Conduct pre-outage coordination meeting with operations, safety, environmental, quality control, and affected trades
- Shut down, drain, depressurize, purge, ventilate, decontaminate, isolate, and lock out equipment; verify documented zero-energy state
- Open, clean, inspect, and measure accessible internal and rotating components; complete specified nondestructive examination
- Repair or replace impellers, rotors, seals, bearings, filters, media, internals, coatings, linings, nozzles, drives, and instruments as applicable
- Reassemble equipment with verified clearances, alignment, torque, gaskets, lubrication, cleanliness, and material compatibility
- Perform pressure, vacuum, leak, rotation, vibration, electrical, calibration, alarm, permissive, and safety-interlock tests as applicable
- Complete controlled startup and verify capacity, pressure, flow, temperature, vibration, leakage, product quality, and operating stability
- Correct deficiencies, reinspect, preserve exposed surfaces, and update maintenance records, spares, warranties, and turnover documents

## 43 05 00 — Common Work Results for Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment

- Confirm process fluid composition, capacity, pressure, temperature, materials, cleanliness, redundancy, utility, and environmental criteria for 43 05 00 Common Work Results for Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment
- Field-verify equipment space, access, maintenance clearances, foundations, routes, penetrations, drains, vents, and tie-in conditions
- Coordinate process, structural, mechanical, electrical, controls, fire-protection, hazardous-area, ventilation, and safety interfaces
- Complete delegated engineering, code analysis, relief review, hazard review, and authority approvals required for the equipment package
- Prepare and obtain approval of product data, certified drawings, calculations, control narratives, testing plans, and manufacturer qualifications
- Release long-lead equipment, specialty alloys, seals, coatings, linings, media, controls, and accessories
- Complete factory quality inspections, material traceability, weld examination, pressure testing, balancing, and factory acceptance testing
- Verify foundations, anchors, secondary steel, access platforms, containment, drainage, and utility rough-ins are ready
- Receive, inspect, preserve, and stage equipment; protect openings, internals, bearings, seals, coatings, linings, and controls
- Execute approved rigging plan and set, level, align, anchor, grout, assemble, and protect equipment
- Connect process piping, utilities, power, grounding, controls, reliefs, vents, drains, guarding, insulation, and identification
- Complete inspections, testing, startup support, deficiency correction, training, spare-parts delivery, and record turnover

## 43 05 05 — Selective Demolition for Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment

- Survey existing equipment, supports, utilities, hazardous contents, retained systems, and demolition limits for 43 05 05 Selective Demolition for Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment
- Confirm shutdown windows, temporary process service, owner salvage requirements, decontamination standards, and waste streams
- Prepare demolition, engineered-lift, isolation, purge, ventilation, environmental-control, and permitting plans
- Identify, label, and protect equipment, piping, controls, wiring, structures, fire barriers, and finishes to remain
- Verify drain-down, depressurization, purge, decontamination, gas testing, lockout/tagout, and documented zero-energy state
- Disconnect process, utility, relief, vent, drain, power, grounding, and control connections at approved boundaries
- Dismantle and remove equipment, media, internals, coatings, linings, supports, and accessories in the approved sequence
- Rig, lower, transport, salvage, recycle, or dispose of removed materials with required manifests and chain-of-custody records
- Cap, blind, seal, label, and pressure- or leak-test abandoned and retained connections
- Patch penetrations, repair substrates and fire barriers, and make the area safe for successor work
- Inspect retained systems and verify they remain clean, protected, and operable after demolition
- Complete deficiency correction, housekeeping, salvage reconciliation, disposal records, and demolition closeout documentation

## 43 06 00 — Schedules for Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment

- Compile the equipment register and applicable asset tags for 43 06 00 Schedules for Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment
- Record service fluid, composition, cleanliness class, process duty, capacity, operating range, and redundancy classification
- Record design and operating pressures, temperatures, flows, differential pressure, NPSH, storage volume, and allowable leakage
- Record power, fuel, cooling, seal, lubrication, purge, instrument-air, drainage, venting, and other utility requirements
- Record dimensions, weights, service clearances, foundation reactions, rigging points, and access requirements
- Record materials, coatings, linings, seals, hazardous-area classification, relief, emissions, and environmental requirements
- Record controls architecture, points, alarms, permissives, trips, sequences, and communication interfaces
- Identify submittal, approval, release, fabrication, factory-test, delivery, setting, tie-in, and startup milestones
- Identify owner-furnished items, loose components, commissioning tools, consumables, media, lubricants, and spare parts
- Coordinate schedule data with P&IDs, layouts, electrical loads, control drawings, asset registers, and procurement logs
- Update schedule data after substitutions, field changes, factory tests, cleaning, pressure tests, and startup
- Validate final installed asset data and issue the approved schedule with operating and maintenance records

## 43 08 00 — Commissioning of Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment

- Develop the commissioning plan, system boundaries, roles, prerequisites, test forms, hazards, and acceptance criteria for 43 08 00 Commissioning of Process Gas and Liquid Handling, Purification, and Storage Equipment
- Review approved submittals, P&IDs, sequences, cause-and-effect matrices, relief design, hazard reviews, and manufacturer requirements
- Verify mechanical completion, access, guarding, cleanliness, lubrication, alignment, coatings, linings, identification, and preservation release
- Verify utilities, flushing, cleaning, pressure or vacuum tests, purge, decontamination, media loading, and environmental permits are complete
- Complete electrical insulation, grounding, phase, rotation, point-to-point, calibration, loop, alarm, permissive, trip, and fail-safe checks
- Witness manufacturer pre-start inspection and authorize controlled energization and introduction of process fluids
- Perform no-load or water-run startup and record vibration, temperature, pressure, flow, differential pressure, sound, and electrical readings
- Execute integrated controls, emergency shutdown, loss-of-utility, gas detection, relief, containment, and equipment-protection tests
- Perform loaded capacity, efficiency, pressure, flow, purity, separation, filtration, storage, or product-quality tests as applicable
- Verify leakage, emissions, waste streams, backwash, regeneration, containment, drainage, venting, and environmental performance
- Correct deficiencies, repeat failed tests, complete deferred tests, and obtain acceptance signatures
- Train operators and maintainers; deliver reports, settings, backups, certificates, spares, warranties, and commissioning records

- Verify foundations, anchors, containment, utility rough-ins, adjacent-system cleanliness, access, and predecessor readiness
- Execute the rigging plan; set, level, align, anchor, grout, assemble, connect, guard, identify, and protect equipment
- Complete support and anchor survey, rigging, setting, alignment, nozzle-fit check, cleaning, pressure or vacuum test, leak test, relief certification, and calibration
- Start, load, and verify pressure and vacuum integrity, leakage, fill and withdrawal rate, level and pressure measurement, relief response, containment, and operating range; correct deficiencies, train personnel, and deliver spares, records, warranties, and turnover documents
